theorem{
  name: refines_bool_not,
  parameters: [x : Bool],
  statement: ((RustSemantics::runExport 12 (coreCrate) "bool_not" (cons (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool x) (nil TargetSyntax::Value))) = (RustSemantics::runItem (ctor RustSyntax::Profile.core) (ctor RustSyntax::Item.boolNot) (cons (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool x) (nil TargetSyntax::Value)))),
  proof: cases{
    branches: [
      {proof: reflexivity{}, binders: [], constructor: "false"},
      {proof: reflexivity{}, binders: [], constructor: "true"}
    ],
    scrutinee: x
  },
  axioms: [Classical.choice, Quot.sound, propext]
}

theorem{
  name: refines_bool_and,
  parameters: [x : Bool, y : Bool],
  statement: ((RustSemantics::runExport 12 (coreCrate) "bool_and" (cons (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool x) (cons (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool y) (nil TargetSyntax::Value)))) = (RustSemantics::runItem (ctor RustSyntax::Profile.core) (ctor RustSyntax::Item.boolAnd) (cons (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool x) (cons (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool y) (nil TargetSyntax::Value))))),
  proof: cases{
    branches: [
      {
        proof: cases{
          branches: [
            {proof: reflexivity{}, binders: [], constructor: "false"},
            {proof: reflexivity{}, binders: [], constructor: "true"}
          ],
          scrutinee: y
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: "false"
      },
      {
        proof: cases{
          branches: [
            {proof: reflexivity{}, binders: [], constructor: "false"},
            {proof: reflexivity{}, binders: [], constructor: "true"}
          ],
          scrutinee: y
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: "true"
      }
    ],
    scrutinee: x
  },
  axioms: [Classical.choice, Quot.sound, propext]
}
